Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on synthesis structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 841. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 854 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the synthesis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 841 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 854. QED.
